Fneu, starts, stops):\n   186\t    \"\"\"dF/F exactly as reward_relative.preprocessing.dff(..., 'maximin', 'subtract').\n   187\t\n   188\t    Samples outside trials are NaN (keep_teleports=False behaviour). Baseline is\n   189\t    computed within each trial: gaussian smoothing (sigma 15 frames), minimum filter\n   190\t    then maximum filter over 300 frames (~20 s), dff = (F - base) / |base|, then\n   191\t    smoothed with a 2-sample gaussian kernel within each trial.\n   192\t    \"\"\"\n   193\t    f_ = np.full(F.shape, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   194\t    fneu_ = np.full(F.shape, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   195\t    for s, e in zip(starts, stops):\n   196\t        f_[:, s:e] = F[:, s:e]\n   197\t        fneu_[:, s:e] = Fneu[:, s:e]\n   198\t\n   199\t    # neuropil subtraction\n   200\t    f_ -= NEU_COEF * fneu_\n   201\t\n   202\t    flow = np.full(F.shape, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   203\t    for s, e in zip(starts, stops):\n   204\t        # add back the per-trial mean neuropil so dF/F is close to true dF/F\n   205\t        f_[:, s:e] += NEU_COEF * np.nanmean(fneu_[:, s:e], axis=1, keepdims=True)\n   206\t        seg = nansmooth(f_[:, s:e], BASELINE_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=-1)\n   207\t        seg = ndimage.minimum_filter1d(seg, BASELINE_FILTER_WIN, axis=-1)\n   208\t        seg = ndimage.maximum_filter1d(seg, BASELINE_FILTER_WIN, axis=-1)\n   209\t        flow[:, s:e] = seg\n   210\t\n   211\t    mask = ~np.isnan(f_[0, :])\n   212\t    dff = np.full(F.shape, np.nan, dtype=np.float32)\n   213\t    dff[:, mask] = (f_[:, mask] - flow[:, mask]) / np.abs(flow[:, mask])\n   214\t\n   215\t    for s, e in zip(starts, stops):\n   216\t        dff[:, s:e] = nansmooth(dff[:, s:e], DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n   217\t    return dff\n   218\t\n   219\t\n   220\tdef deconvolve(dff, starts, stops):\n   221\t    \"\"\"OASIS deconvolution of dF/F per trial (reference: dcnv.oasis in dff()).\"\"\"\n   222\t    from suite2p.extraction import dcnv  # lazy import: keeps OpenMP out of the parent\n   223\t    spks = np.zeros(dff.shape, dtype=np.float32)\n   224\t    for s, e in zip(starts, stops):\n   225\t        spks[:, s:e] = dcnv.oasis(\n   226\t            np.ascontiguousarray(dff[:, s:e], dtype=np.float32),\n   227\t            2000, TAU, FRAME_RATE)\n   228\t    return spks\n   229\t\n   230\t\n   231\t# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- session\n   232\tdef convert_session(fname, show_processing=False, neural_signal='dff'):\n   233\t    \"\"\"Convert one NWB session. Returns a dict (or None if nothing usable).\"\"\"\n   234\t    t0 = time.time()\n   235\t    timings = {}\n   236\t    with h5py.File(fname, 'r') as f:\n   237\t        scene = f['identifier'][()].decode().split('/')[-1]\n   238\t        subject = f['general/subject/subject_id'][()].decode()\n   239\t        session_id = f['general/session_id'][()].decode()\n   240\t        region = f['general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/location'][()].decode()\n   241\t        rate = float(f['general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/imaging_rate'][()])\n   242\t        beh = load_behavior(f)\n   243\t        t1 = time.time()\n   244\t        F, Fneu, plane_idx = load_fluorescence(f)\n   245\t    timings['load'] = time.time() - t0\n   246\t\n   247\t    # Occasionally the VR/behavior stream is 1 sample longer than the imaging stream\n   248\t    # (\"one frame correction ... scan stopping mid frame\" in the reference\n   249\t    # TwoPUtils/preprocessing.vr_align_to_2P). Truncate both to the common length.\n   250\t    n_beh = len(beh['position'])\n   251\t    n_ophys = F.shape[1]\n   252\t    nframes = min(n_beh, n_ophys)\n   253\t    if n_beh != n_ophys:\n   254\t        assert abs(n_beh - n_ophys) <= 5, (n_beh, n_ophys)\n   255\t        print(f'  {os.path.basename(fname)}: behavior has {n_beh} samples, imaging '\n   256\t              f'{n_ophys}; truncating both to {nframes} (reference one-frame correction)')\n   257\t        for k in list(beh.keys()):\n   258\t            beh[k] = beh[k][:nframes]\n   259\t        F = F[:, :nframes]\n   260\t        Fneu = Fneu[:, :nframes]\n   261\t\n   262\t    # ---- trials: [trial_start, teleport); the teleport frame has an interpolated position\n   263\t    starts = np.where(beh['trial_start'] > 0)[0]\n   264\t    stops = np.where(beh['teleport'] > 0)[0]\n   265\t    assert len(starts) == len(stops) and np.all(stops > starts)\n   266\t    # a trial whose teleport was cut off by the truncation above is dropped\n   267\t    valid = stops < nframes\n   268\t    if not np.all(valid):\n   269\t        print(f'  {os.path.basename(fname)}: dropping {int((~valid).sum())} trial(s) '\n   270\t              f'truncated at the end of the recording')\n   271\t        starts, stops = starts[valid], stops[valid]\n   272\t    ntrials_raw = len(starts)\n   273\t\n   274\t    # ---- dF/F and deconvolved activity (the paper's `events`)\n   275\t    t1 = time.time()\n   276\t    dff = compute_dff(F, Fneu, starts, stops)\n   277\t    timings['dff'] = time.time() - t1\n   278\t\n   279\t    # ---- neuron curation: exclude putative interneurons (r(dF/F, speed) > 0.5)\n   280\t    in_trial = np.zeros(nframes, dtype=bool)\n   281\t    for s, e in zip(starts, stops):\n   282\t        in_trial[s:e] = True\n   283\t    d = dff[:, in_trial]\n   284\t    sp = beh['speed'][in_trial].astype(np.float32)\n   285\t    dz = d - d.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n   286\t    sz = sp - sp.mean()\n   287\t    denom = (np.sqrt((dz ** 2).sum(axis=1)) * np.sqrt((sz ** 2).sum()))\n   288\t    with np.errstate(invalid='ignore', divide='ignore'):\n   289\t        r_speed = (dz @ sz) / denom\n   290\t    r_speed = np.nan_to_num(r_speed, nan=0.0)\n   291\t    keep_cells = r_speed <= INTERNEURON_R_THRESH\n   292\t    n_interneurons = int((~keep_cells).sum())\n   293\t\n   294\t    t1 = time.time()\n   295\t    dff_kept = dff[keep_cells]\n   296\t    events = deconvolve(dff_kept, starts, stops)\n   297\t    timings['deconv'] = time.time() - t1\n   298\t    activity = events if neural_signal == 'events' else np.nan_to_num(dff_kept)\n   299\t\n   300\t    # ---- per-trial task variables\n   301\t    zones, envs_scene = zone_per_trial(scene, ntrials_raw)\n   302\t    env_stream = np.array([np.median(beh['environment'][s:e])\n   303\t                           for s, e in zip(starts, stops)])\n   304\t    # reward outcome, as in behavior.get_trial_types: reward delivered AND zone entered\n   305\t    isreward = np.array([(beh['reward'][s:e].sum() > 0) and\n   306\t                         (beh['reward_zone'][s:e].sum() > 0)\n   307\t                         for s, e in zip(starts, stops)], dtype=np.int64)\n   308\t    # lick-sensor error trials (paper: >30% of frames with cumulative lick count > 2)\n   309\t    lick_bad = np.array([(beh['lick'][s:e] > LICK_ERROR_COUNT).mean() > LICK_ERROR_FRAC\n   310\t                         for s, e in zip(starts, stops)])\n   311\t\n   312\t    neural, inputs, outputs = [], [], []\n   313\t    kept_trials = []\n   314\t    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(zip(starts, stops)):\n   315\t        if lick_bad[i]:\n   316\t            continue\n   317\t        T = e - s\n   318\t        pos = beh['position'][s:e].astype(np.float64)\n   319\t        speed = beh['speed'][s:e].astype(np.float64)\n   320\t        licks = beh['lick'][s:e].astype(np.float64)\n   321\t        tt = beh['t'][s:e] - beh['t'][s]\n   322\t\n   323\t        zone = REWARD_ZONES[zones[i]]\n   324\t        dist = signed_distance_to_zone(pos, zone)\n   325\t\n   326\t        inp = np.empty((len(INPUT_NAMES), T), dtype=np.float32)\n   327\t        inp[0] = tt\n   328\t        inp[1] = env_stream[i]\n   329\t        inp[2] = i\n   330\t        inp[3] = isreward[i - 1] if i > 0 else 1   # see notes: warm-up trials precede\n   331\t\n   332\t        out = np.empty((len(OUTPUT_NAMES), T), dtype=np.int64)\n   333\t        out[0] = bin_distance(dist)\n   334\t        out[1] = bin_position(pos)\n   335\t        out[2] = bin_speed(speed)\n   336\t        out[3] = (licks > 0).astype(np.int64)\n   337\t        out[4] = ZONE_TO_IDX[zones[i]]\n   338\t        out[5] = isreward[i]\n   339\t\n   340\t        act = np.ascontiguousarray(activity[:, s:e], dtype=np.float32)\n   341\t        assert np.all(np.isfinite(act)) and np.all(np.isfinite(inp))\n   342\t        neural.append(act)\n   343\t        inputs.append(inp)\n   344\t        outputs.append(out)\n   345\t        kept_trials.append(i)\n   346\t\n   347\t    if len(neural) < 2:\n   348\t        print(f'  !! {os.path.basename(fname)}: only {len(neural)} usable trials, skipping')\n   349\t        return None\n   350\t\n   351\t    result = dict(\n   352\t        file=os.path.basename(fname), subject=subject, session_id=session_id,\n   353\t        scene=scene, region=region, imaging_rate=rate,\n   354\t        neural=neural, input=inputs, output=outputs,\n   355\t        n_neurons=int(events.shape[0]), n_interneurons=n_interneurons,\n   356\t        n_roi_iscell=int(F.shape[0]), plane_idx=plane_idx[keep_cells],\n   357\t        ntrials_raw=ntrials_raw, kept_trials=np.array(kept_trials),\n   358\t        n_lick_bad=int(lick_bad.sum()),\n   359\t        isreward=isreward, zones=zones, env_stream=env_stream, envs_scene=envs_scene,\n   360\t        timings=timings, total_time=time.time() - t0,\n   361\t    )\n   362\t\n   363\t    if show_processing:\n   364\t        plot_processing(result, beh, F, Fneu, dff_kept, events, starts, stops)\n   365\t\n   366\t    print(f'  {os.path.basename(fname)}: {result[\"n_neurons\"]} cells '\n   367\t          f'({n_interneurons} interneurons excluded), {len(neural)}/{ntrials_raw} trials '\n   368\t          f'({int(lick_bad.sum())} lick-error), {result[\"total_time\"]:.1f} s '\n   369\t          f'(load {timings[\"load\"]:.1f}, dff {timings[\"dff\"]:.1f}, '\n   370\t          f'deconv {timings[\"deconv\"]:.1f})', flush=True)\n   371\t    return result\n   372\t\n   373\t\n   374\t# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- plotting\n   375\tdef plot_processing(res, beh, F, Fneu, dff, events, starts, stops):\n   376\t    \"\"\"Visualize every processing step for one session.\"\"\"\n   377\t    import matplotlib\n   378\t    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n   379\t    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n   380\t\n   381\t    sid = f'{res[\"subject\"]}_ses{res[\"session_id\"]}'\n   382\t    fig, ax = plt.subplots(7, 1, figsize=(16, 20), sharex=True)\n   383\t    # first 6 trials\n   384\t    s0, s1 = starts[0], stops[min(5, len(stops) - 1)]\n   385\t    tt = beh['t'][s0:s1]\n   386\t    ncell = min(4, F.shape[0])\n   387\t    for c in range(ncell):\n   388\t        ax[0].plot(tt, F[c, s0:s1], lw=0.6, label=f'F cell{c}')\n   389\t        ax[0].plot(tt, Fneu[c, s0:s1], lw=0.4, alpha=0.5)\n   390\t    ax[0].set_ylabel('raw F / Fneu')\n   391\t    ax[0].legend(fontsize=6, ncol=4)\n   392\t    ax[0].set_title(f'{sid} ({res[\"scene\"]}): processing steps, first 6 trials')\n   393\t    for c in range(min(4, dff.shape[0])):\n   394\t        ax[1].plot(tt, dff[c, s0:s1], lw=0.6)\n   395\t    ax[1].set_ylabel('dF/F (maximin)')\n   396\t    for c in range(min(4, events.shape[0])):\n   397\t        ax[2].plot(tt, events[c, s0:s1], lw=0.6)\n   398\t    ax[2].set_ylabel('deconvolved (events)')\n   399\t    ax[3].plot(tt, beh['position'][s0:s1], 'k', lw=1)\n   400\t    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(zip(starts, stops)):\n   401\t        if s >= s1:\n   402\t            break\n   403\t        ax[3].axvline(beh['t'][s], color='g', lw=0.8)\n   404\t        ax[3].axvline(beh['t'][e], color='r', lw=0.8, ls='--')\n   405\t        lo, hi = REWARD_ZONES[res['zones'][i]]\n   406\t        ax[3].fill_between([beh['t'][s], beh['t'][e - 1]], lo, hi, color='gold', alpha=.4)\n   407\t    ax[3].set_ylabel('position (cm)\\n+reward zone')\n   408\t    ax[4].plot(tt, beh['speed'][s0:s1], 'b', lw=1)\n   409\t    ax[4].axhline(2, color='k', ls=':')\n   410\t    ax[4].set_ylabel('speed (cm/s)')\n   411\t    ax[5].plot(tt, beh['lick'][s0:s1], 'm', lw=1)\n   412\t    ax[5].plot(tt, beh['reward'][s0:s1] * 5, 'c', lw=1)\n   413\t    ax[5].set_ylabel('lick count / reward')\n   414\t    # distance to zone and its binning\n   415\t    dist_all = np.full(len(beh['position']), np.nan)\n   416\t    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(zip(starts, stops)):\n   417\t        dist_all[s:e] = signed_distance_to_zone(beh['position'][s:e],\n   418\t                                                REWARD_ZONES[res['zones'][i]])\n   419\t    ax[6].plot(tt, dist_all[s0:s1], 'k', lw=1)\n   420\t    ax6b = ax[6].twinx()\n   421\t    db = np.full(len(tt), np.nan)\n   422\t    m = ~np.isnan(dist_all[s0:s1])\n   423\t    db[m] = bin_distance(dist_all[s0:s1][m])\n   424\t    ax6b.plot(tt, db, 'r.', ms=2)\n   425\t    ax6b.set_ylabel('distance bin (red)')\n   426\t    ax[6].set_ylabel('distance to reward zone (cm)')\n   427\t    ax[6].set_xlabel('time (s)')\n   428\t    fig.tight_layout()\n   429\t    fig.savefig(f'processing_{sid}.png', dpi=110)\n   430\t    plt.close(fig)\n   431\t\n   432\t    # --- second figure: converted trial matrices + discretization checks\n   433\t    fig, ax = plt.subplots(4, 3, figsize=(18, 12))\n   434\t    for j, tr in enumerate([0, 1, min(len(res['neural']) - 1, 40)]):\n   435\t        act = res['neural'][tr]\n   436\t        inp = res['input'][tr]\n   437\t        out = res['output'][tr]\n   438\t        ax[0, j].imshow(act[:min(80, act.shape[0])], aspect='auto',\n   439\t                        interpolation='nearest', cmap='magma')\n   440\t        ax[0, j].set_title(f'trial {tr} (orig {res[\"kept_trials\"][tr]}): events')\n   441\t        for k in range(inp.shape[0]):\n   442\t            ax[1, j].plot(inp[k], label=INPUT_NAMES[k])\n   443\t        ax[1, j].legend(fontsize=6)\n   444\t        ax[1, j].set_title('inputs')\n   445\t        for k in range(out.shape[0]):\n   446\t            ax[2, j].step(np.arange(out.shape[1]), out[k] + 8 * k, where='post',\n   447\t                          label=OUTPUT_NAMES[k])\n   448\t        ax[2, j].legend(fontsize=6)\n   449\t        ax[2, j].set_title('outputs (offset by 8)')\n   450\t        # verify discretization: position vs position bin\n   451\t        s, e = starts[res['kept_trials'][tr]], stops[res['kept_trials'][tr]]\n   452\t        ax[3, j].plot(beh['position'][s:e], 'k', lw=1, label='pos (cm)')\n   453\t        ax[3, j].plot(out[1] * 90, 'r--', lw=1, label='pos bin x 90')\n   454\t        z = REWARD_ZONES[res['zones'][res['kept_trials'][tr]]]\n   455\t        ax[3, j].axhspan(z[0], z[1], color='gold', alpha=.3)\n   456\t        ax[3, j].plot(np.where(out[0] == 3, 450, np.nan), 'g.', ms=3,\n   457\t                      label='in-zone bin')\n   458\t        ax[3, j].legend(fontsize=6)\n   459\t        ax[3, j].set_xlabel('frame in trial')\n   460\t    fig.suptitle(f'{sid} converted trials')\n   461\t    fig.tight_layout()\n   462\t    fig.savefig(f'processing_{sid}_trials.png', dpi=110)\n   463\t    plt.close(fig)\n   464\t\n   465\t\n   466\t# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- driver\n   467\tdef _worker(args):\n   468\t    fname, show, neural_signal = args\n   469\t    warnings.simplefilter('ignore', category=RuntimeWarning)\n   470\t    try:\n   471\t        return convert_session(fname, show_processing=show, neural_signal=neural_signal)\n   472\t    except Exception as exc:  # keep going, but report loudly\n   473\t        import traceback\n   474\t        print(f'  !! FAILED {fname}: {exc}')\n   475\t        traceback.print_exc()\n   476\t        return None\n   477\t\n   478\t\n   479\tdef main():\n   480\t    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)\n   481\t    ap.add_argument('outfile')\n   482\t    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True)\n   483\t    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true',\n   484\t                    help='process only 2 sessions (for testing)')\n   485\t    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true',\n   486\t                    help='save per-step processing plots for up to 2 sessions')\n   487\t    ap.add_argument('--nproc', type=int, default=8)\n   488\t    ap.add_argument('--neural-signal', choices=['events', 'dff'], default='dff',\n   489\t                    help=\"neural stream to save: 'events' = deconvolved dF/F (paper's \"\n   490\t                         \"default for population analyses), 'dff' = dF/F\")\n   491\t    ap.add_argument('--sessions', type=str, default=None,\n   492\t                    help='comma-separated substrings selecting specific sessions')\n   493\t    ap.add_argument('--data-dir', default='/app/data')\n   494\t    args = ap.parse_args()\n   495\t\n   496\t    files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(args.data_dir, 'sub-*', '*.nwb')))\n   497\t    if args.sessions:\n   498\t        pats = args.sessions.split(',')\n   499\t        files = [f for f in files if any(p in f for p in pats)]\n   500\t    if args.sample:\n   501\t        # one small single-plane session and one large two-plane session\n   502\t        pick = [f for f in files if 'sub-m11_ses-03' in f or 'sub-m17_ses-08' in f]\n   503\t        files = pick if len(pick) == 2 else files[:2]\n   504\t    print(f'Converting {len(files)} sessions with {args.nproc} workers')\n   505\t\n   506\t    show_n = 2 if args.show_processing else 0\n   507\t    jobs = [(f, i < show_n, args.neural_signal) for i, f in enumerate(files)]\n   508\t\n   509\t    t0 = time.time()\n   510\t    results = []\n   511\t    if args.nproc > 1 and len(files) > 1:\n   512\t        ctx = mp.get_context('spawn')\n   513\t        with ctx.Pool(args.nproc, maxtasksperchild=1) as pool:\n   514\t            for i, res in enumerate(pool.imap(_worker, jobs)):\n   515\t                if res is not None:\n   516\t                    results.append(res)\n   517\t                el = time.time() - t0\n   518\t                print(f'[{i+1}/{len(files)}] elapsed {el/60:.1f} min, '\n   519\t                      f'projected total {el/(i+1)*len(files)/60:.1f} min', flush=True)\n   520\t    else:\n   521\t        for i, job in enumerate(jobs):\n   522\t            res = _worker(job)\n   523\t            if res is not None:\n   524\t                results.append(res)\n   525\t            el = time.time() - t0\n   526\t            print(f'[{i+1}/{len(files)}] elapsed {el/60:.1f} min, '\n   527\t                  f'projected total {el/(i+1)*len(files)/60:.1f} min', flush=True)\n   528\t\n   529\t    print(f'Converted {len(results)} sessions in {(time.time()-t0)/60:.1f} min')\n   530\t\n   531\t    # ---- assemble the target structure\n   532\t    results.sort(key=lambda r: (r['subject'], r['session_id']))\n   533\t    subjects = sorted({r['subject'] for r in results},\n   534\t                      key=lambda s: int(re.sub(r'\\D', '', s)))\n   535\t    brain_regions = ['CA1']\n   536\t\n   537\t    data = {\n   538\t        'neural': [r['neural'] for r in results],\n   539\t        'input': [r['input'] for r in results],\n   540\t        'output': [r['output'] for r in results],\n   541\t        'subjects': subjects,\n   542\t        'subject_idx': np.array([subjects.index(r['subject']) for r in results],\n   543\t                                dtype=np.int64),\n   544\t        'brain_regions': brain_regions,\n   545\t        'brain_region_idx': [np.zeros(r['n_neurons'], dtype=np.int64) for r in results],\n   546\t        'input_names': INPUT_NAMES,\n   547\t        'output_names': OUTPUT_NAMES,\n   548\t        'output_values': OUTPUT_VALUES,\n   549\t        'metadata': {\n   550\t            'task_description': (\n   551\t                'Head-fixed mice run laps on a 450 cm virtual linear track with a hidden '\n   552\t                '50 cm reward zone at one of three locations (A 80-130, B 200-250, '\n   553\t                'C 320-370 cm) in one of two visually distinct environments (ENV1/ENV2). '\n   554\t                'Reward is delivered operantly for licking in the zone and randomly '\n   555\t                'omitted on ~15% of trials; on switch days the zone (and on day 8/14 the '\n   556\t                'environment) changes after 30 trials. Decoder outputs: distance to the '\n   557\t                'reward zone, absolute track position, running speed, licking, reward zone '\n   558\t                'identity and trial reward outcome. Fixed by importing `dcnv` lazily inside the worker and using a `spawn` Pool.\n- reading `Fluorescence` with h5py per-plane and transposing once is much faster than per-cell reads.\n- the Pearson r(dF/F, speed) loop over cells was replaced by a single matrix product.\n\nCode speedups added:\n- 8 parallel worker processes (one session each).\n- float32 throughout; per-trial slices are contiguous copies only once.\n\n---\n\n## Step 7: Sample Conversion and Validation\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\nCommand: `python -u /app/convert_data.py /app/sample_data.pkl --sample --show-processing`\n(sessions sub-m11_ses-03, the smallest single-plane session, and sub-m17_ses-08, a two-plane\nsession with an in-session environment switch).\n\n### Sample Statistics\n| Statistic | Value |\n|-----------|-------|\n| Sessions | 2 |\n| Neurons (total) | 741 (154 + 587) |\n| Neurons / session | 154, 587 (7 putative interneurons excluded = 0.94%) |\n| Subjects | 2 (m11, m17) |\n| Sessions / subject | 1 |\n| Trials (total) | 160 |\n| Trials / session | 80, 80 (0 lick-error trials in these sessions) |\n| T per trial | mean 190.6 frames, min 137, max 338 |\n| time_from_trial_start range | [0.0, 21.7] s |\n| environment range | [0, 1] |\n| trial_number range | [0, 79] |\n| previous_trial_outcome range | [0, 1] |\n| reward_zone_distance distribution | [0.138, 0.094, 0.042, 0.261, 0.023, 0.082, 0.360] |\n| position distribution | [0.257, 0.193, 0.252, 0.160, 0.138] |\n| speed distribution | [0.068, 0.070, 0.090, 0.326, 0.446] |\n| lick distribution | [0.823, 0.177] |\n| reward_zone_location distribution | [0.573, 0.427, 0.0] (A and B only in these 2 sessions) |\n| reward_outcome distribution | [0.116, 0.884] |\n| neural signal | dF/F (`metadata['neural_signal_type'] = 'dff'`), range e.g. [-0.12, 2.14] on trial 0 |\n\nChecks: the in-zone class (distance bin 3) covers 26% of samples, consistent with a 50 cm zone\nplus slow running inside it;
